V3 Mainline Caller Flow

Canonical Markdown source: docs/architecture/wiki/v3-mainline-caller-flow.md Edge source: docs/architecture/v3-mainline-call-map.yml 58 functional paths 376 caller edges

Request skeleton / 请求主骨架

请求主骨架只显示 Hub Relay 的真实 edge：inbound → continuation classify → Chat Process → execution/target → outbound → compat → provider wire/transport。VR/Target 属于 request chain 的 execution/target 阶段，provider outbound 必须经过 compat。

%%{init: {"flowchart": {"htmlLabels": true, "curve": "basis", "rankSpacing": 86, "nodeSpacing": 46}, "themeVariables": {"fontSize": "18px"}} }%%

  rq_1["<b>ReqInbound 归一化</b><br/>只做入口协议解析/非破坏性归一化<br/><small>V3HubReqInbound02Normalized</small>"]
  rq_2["<b>Continuation 分类</b><br/>只判定 scope/owner/entry；不恢复错误路径<br/><small>V3HubReqContinuation03Classified</small>"]

Response skeleton / 响应主骨架

响应主骨架只显示 provider 回来后的真实 Relay edge：provider raw → response compat → RespInbound → Resp03 Chat Process → Resp04 continuation save → RespOutbound → Server frame。响应治理只能在 Resp03；Resp04 只保存 continuation。

Read format / 阅读格式

图节点

人读标题 → 一句话职责 → contract node id。审计时先看职责是否在正确层，再看 raw node id。

图边

step_id → 语义动作。高风险边会写专用注释；普通边显示 caller module → callee module。

表格

表格保留真实 caller_symbol / callee_symbol / source file，用来回源代码验证，不让图承担所有细节。

Side-channel meaning / side-channel 含义

它不是资源

side-channel 是控制事实的传输方式，不是资源名、不是 payload 字段、也不是第二套 request/response。

真正资源/owner

这里的资源是 Error handler、provider health state、debug/error envelope 等明确 owner。HTML 的 Error resources 区块单独展示这些 owner/resource。

允许做法

主链节点可以把错误、health、debug 事实写给 Error handler / health owner；这些事实只影响错误投影、健康状态、路由可用性。

禁止做法

不得把 debug/error/metadata/control 字段塞进 provider request body 或 client response body；不得用 side-channel 重建业务 payload。

Error resources / 错误处理资源

错误链是独立资源链：错误进入 V3Error01SourceRaised 后逐节点分类、策略、执行决策、客户端投影。右侧两个 provider 节点不是错误来源：V3Transport13ResponsesHttpRequest 表示 provider send/transport 失败时写 health failure，V3ProviderResp14Raw 表示收到 raw 响应后写 health success；它们只服务 provider health/availability。

Review status legend

typed-test-only

Typed skeleton/builder edge from test-owned contract evidence; it proves node topology, not live runtime closure.

live-bound

Real source symbols are bound in Rust runtime/source files; live replay remains a separate required proof.

aggregate-only

Wrapper/entry edge only. It must not be read as an adjacent semantic pipeline transition.

binding_pending

Map owner is declared but caller/callee or source evidence is not yet strong enough to claim closure.

runtime gap

Any typed-test-only, aggregate-only, or binding_pending edge is a visible runtime gap until a runtime worker binds and verifies the adjacent owner path.

v 3.sse.transport_boundary V3SseTransportIn01RawChunk → V3ServerRespOutbound06ClientFrame 4 edges · v3.sse.transport_boundary · V3SseTransportIn01RawChunk → V3ServerRespOutbound06ClientFrame

V3 SSE is a transport-only edge: provider bytes become opaque validated SSE frames in routecodex-v3-sse, protocol semantics are handled by provider/protocol codecs, and server output only hands finalized client stream bytes to Body::from_stream.

owner: v3.sse_transport_core_independent audit: locked by Jason modules: routecodex-v3-sse · v3-provider-responses · v3-server resources: 6 manifest: docs/architecture/manifests/v3.sse.protocol_codec_projection.mainline.yml

  n_v3_sse_transport_boundary_1["<b>SSE decoded frame</b><br/>只完成 UTF-8、line、frame 字段解码；data JSON 仍保持 opaque<br/><small>V3SseTransportIn02DecodedFrame</small>"]

v 3.protocol_conversion_field_parity V3HubReqInbound01ClientRaw → V3ServerRespOutbound06ClientFrame 8 edges · v3.protocol_conversion_field_parity · V3HubReqInbound01ClientRaw → V3ServerRespOutbound06ClientFrame

Field-parity contract overlay over existing V3 Relay chains. Source wire is decoded to Chat canonical plus registered payload extensions before Chat Process; outbound projection consumes only governed Chat semantics. Arguments, client metadata, reasoning effort/budget/summary/context/mode/include/display, and other payload fields stay in the data plane. Routing/switch/retry/continuation control stays in typed control resources/MetadataCenter, and neither side reconstructs the other. It does not introduce a separate runtime lifecycle or any server/SSE/provider-transport owner.

owner: v3.protocol_conversion_field_parity audit: locked by Jason modules: v3-runtime · v3-runtime::hub_v1 resources: 9

v 3.responses_session_admission V3Server03HttpRequestRaw → V3ServerRespOutbound06ClientFrame 3 edges · v3.responses_session_admission · V3Server03HttpRequestRaw → V3ServerRespOutbound06ClientFrame

The listener-local HTTP boundary admits one active Responses request per explicit session or conversation, rejects overlap through Error01-06 before Runtime/provider send, and holds the exact permit until the client response body reaches EOF, errors, or is dropped.

v 3.provider_action_gate.mainline ProviderReqCompat06ProviderCompat → V3ProviderResponsesTerminalOrFailureObserved 49 edges · v3.provider_action_gate.mainline · ProviderReqCompat06ProviderCompat → V3ProviderResponsesTerminalOrFailureObserved

V3 provider action gate serializes provider failure recovery, terminal projection, permit ownership, post-commit SSE success/failure observation, and provider response event-codec terminal evidence without Server/SSE owning Error06 semantics.

owner: v3.provider_action_gate audit: locked by Jason modules: v3-runtime · v3-runtime::hub_v1 resources: 3 manifest: docs/architecture/manifests/v3.provider_action_gate.mainline.yml
